## Document 006: 1Q26兩岸晶圓代工淡季不淡 漲價效應將推升2Q26及2026全年兩岸產業營收_20260520-149

### Page 1

玉山證券投資顧問(股)公司 dannywen0523-72306@esunsec.com.tw 於 26/05/22 07:37 下載
1Q26兩岸晶圓代工淡季不淡
漲價效應將推升2Q26及2026
全年兩岸產業營收
陳澤嘉 、黃健治
DIGITIMES／分析師
DIGITIMES觀察，2026年第1季台灣與中國晶圓代工業營收分別季增6%、1.7%，
達387億美元、41億美元，反映產業淡季不淡，而AI應用與官方政策分別是台灣
與中國業者重要的營收支撐來源。展望第2季，在客戶將消費性電子應用晶片訂
單挪到其他應用，並在晶圓代工漲價效應發酵下，兩岸的產業營收皆將再成長
超過10%，並且預估同步推升2026全年合計營收年增率皆達25%以上。
2026年第1季兩岸晶圓代工產業呈現淡季不淡，台廠部分主要依靠AI應用帶動，
使台積電先進製程持續暢旺，推升台灣晶圓代工業營收季增，若扣除台積電營
收，則其他台廠合計營收達27.5億美元，季減0.9%，其中，隱含非AI應用仍受
電子業傳統淡季影響，但優於過往淡季的營收成長動能；在中國晶圓代工業部

分，受惠半導體自主化政策及中國於2026年初再推動新一輪消費補貼，支撐中
系業者訂單，使中國晶圓代工業第1季營收達41.3億美元，季增1.7%。
展望2026年第2季，雖然消費性電子晶片需求將受記憶體價格飆漲拖累，但在客
戶已將訂單挪往AI、汽車、無人機等應用，因此未見明顯砍單；另一方面，兩
岸業者已陸續在2025年下半至2026年第1季宣布調漲代工價格，漲價效應已於
2026年第1季底開始陸續發酵。DIGITIMES預估，2026年兩岸晶圓代工業第2季
營收皆將季增超過10%。此外，漲價效應也將帶動2026全年營收成長動能，其
中，台灣晶圓代工業營收上看1,770億美元，年增逾3成；中國晶圓代工業營收
1

### Page 2

將挑戰190億美元，年增估達25%。

DDR4記憶體將逐步停產，利基型記憶體代工需求增溫。 ↑★ ↑★★

台積電CoWoS產能擴增。 ↑★ ↑★★

兩岸晶圓代工業者陸續調漲代工價格。 ↑★ ↑★★
1Q26及2Q26影響兩岸晶圓代工營收供需因素
• 兩岸晶圓代工業者營收將受地緣政治、記憶體價格飆漲影響，不
利晶片需求，將成為不利2026年上半兩岸晶圓代工營收的主因。
• 美國對等關稅政策雖在美國高等法院於2026年第1季判決違法後暫
停，美國仍尋求其他法源課徵短期關稅，並影響全球貿易。
• 中美科技競爭使供應鏈持續推動「China+1」、「China for China」
政策，兩岸晶圓代工都有轉單效益。
• 美伊戰爭及後續談判的不確定性已造成地緣動盪及全球通膨升溫，
總體經濟面臨短期壓力，同時，半導體材料也面臨供應影響。
• 半導體材料因美伊戰爭造成供給波動及影響生產成本高漲，成為
兩岸晶圓代工業者推動漲價的原因之一。
• 2026年上半氦氣等關鍵原物料雖無斷供問題，但美伊和談如受阻，
對半導體關鍵材料穩定供應能力的影響將陸續浮現。
• 記憶體價格持續飆漲，中低階消費性電子因生產成本高漲，使業
者取消生產計畫，並拖累相關晶片。
• 記憶體供不應求，部分利基型記憶體代工業者將受惠。

• AI應用與中高階消費性電子產品成為晶片需求的主要支撐，另外，
2

### Page 4

中國官方新一輪消費補貼政策也帶動車用、消費性電子需求，提
供2026年上半晶片需求支撐。
• 雲端服務供應商對運算力軍備競賽延續趨勢已反映在各自的2026
年資本支出規畫，也隱含對AI晶片的需求動能持續維持強勁。
• 台積電持續推動先進製程與先進封裝產能擴張計畫，且宣布2026
年資本支出金額將超過500億美元。
• 手機、NB/PC等消費性電子業者將更聚焦中高階產品生產計畫，
或轉向其他新興需求，如ADAS、車載娛樂系統、無人機等。
• 目前IC設計業者主要以調整各類應用晶片的投片比例取代砍單，
2026年第2季訂單將無虞。
• 中國除持續推動半導體自主化戰略，2026年初中國也推動新一輪
消費補貼政策，除原先涵蓋汽車、家電、手機、NB等產品，也新
納入智慧手環、智慧眼鏡、智慧家居等產品，強化中國市場的晶
片需求。
• 中國晶圓代工業者仍持續推動產能擴張，強化中國自主產能需求。
• 為反應生產成本提升或部分製程強勁需求，兩岸晶圓代工業者都
已陸續在2026年啟動代工價格調漲，將在2026年上半開始反映在

營收。
• 聯電宣布將在2026年下半開始調漲價格，效益將略晚於其他業者。
2

1Q25~1Q26台灣與中國晶圓代工業營收變化
台廠 中企
1Q26 兩岸晶圓代工營收皆呈現淡季不淡 然成因不同

單位：十億美元
• DIGITIMES觀察2026年第1季台廠晶圓代工合計營收狀況，呈現淡季不淡，整
體營收規模提升至387億美元，季增6%，年增38％，AI應用持續帶動台積電
先進製程需求是主因。
• 第1季為電子業傳統淡季，但台廠展現淡季不淡的格局，顯示AI伺服器晶片的
強勁需求可完全抵消消費性電子的傳統淡季效應。
• 受制記憶體價格持續飆漲影響，中低階手機、NB/PC等消費性電子晶片訂單
已移轉至車用、無人機等應用。
• 台積電主導台廠營收走勢，第1季營收季增6%，主要受惠AI伺服器需求持續
強勁。
• 第1季聯電、力積電及世界先進合計營收為27.5億美元，季減0.9%，反映非AI
應用仍受消費性電子傳統淡季影響。
• 2026年第1季中系晶圓代工業者合計營收達41.3億美元，季增1.7%，年增14.1
％，營收成長動能主要來自政策支持。
• 中國持續推動半導體自主化戰略，加上2026年初持續推動購新補貼方案，政
策除提供汽車、家電等產品購新補貼外，也將智慧手環、智慧家居產品納入
補貼範疇，帶動汽車與消費性電子需求。

• 需求包含MCU、利基型記憶體、電源管理IC (PMIC)。
• 中國晶圓代工業者合計營收已持續領先不含台積電的台廠合計營收，凸顯中
國業者持續擴大競爭優勢。
4

• 台廠3奈米製程營收佔比在2025年第4季急遽攀升至26%，主要受
惠於蘋果(Apple)新一代iPhone處理器及其他非蘋陣營旗艦手機應
用處理器(AP)放量，推升3奈米製程的營收貢獻；然而，2026年第
1季比例回落，則是新機鋪貨高峰已過所致。

• 5奈米製程佔比持續維持在32%以上，穩居營收佔比的最大節點，
主要受惠於AI與HPC基礎建設的強勁且持續的需求。
• 7奈米製程佔比較穩定，在車用電子、網通晶片、部分消費性產品
與邊緣AI應用，提供效能與良率的平衡方案，逐漸承接過去28/16
奈米製程的角色。
• 成熟製程方面，40奈米以上製程佔比呈逐季下降的趨勢，主要也
是受到先進製程佔比持續攀升排擠。
• 成熟製程的產能越來越依賴特殊製程的整合來維持毛利，如高壓
製程、BCD電源管理、異質整合、矽光子技術與矽中介層等。

台積電 聯電 力積電 世界先進 中芯國際 華虹半導體 晶合集成
1Q25 2Q25 3Q25 4Q25 1Q26
• 觀察台廠2026年第1季產能利用率變化，多在持平至略增，維持約80%水準；
中系業者產能利用率則顯著高於台廠，達90~100%，維持高檔水準。
• 中國當地需求與國產替代效應仍持續支撐成熟製程產能，使中系晶圓代工業
者持續優於台廠。
• 在地緣政治下，雖然部分業者選擇「China+1」策略，將訂單移轉到台廠，但
台廠也有部分訂單流失，轉向中系業者。
• 在中系業者以低價競爭強化客戶投片意願情勢影響下，中系晶圓代工業者已
持續壓縮台廠成熟製程的接單與報價空間。

• 欲搶佔中國內需市場商機的外商企業，也推動「China for China」投片策略，
推升中系業者產能利用率。

兩岸晶圓代工掀漲價潮 新價格於1Q26 開始陸續反映
資料來源：各業者，DIGITIMES整理，2026/5
業者 宣布時點 漲價幅度 生效時點 挹注營收時點
台積電 3Q25 先進製程：3~10% 1Q26 1Q26
聯電 2Q26 10% 3Q26 4Q26
力積電 1Q26 • 記憶體：20%
• 非記憶體：5~10% 1Q26 2Q26
世界先進 1Q26 8吋：4~8% 2Q26 2Q26
中芯國際 4Q25 部分製程：10% 1Q26 1Q26
華虹集團 1Q26 8吋：5~20% 2Q26 2Q26
晶合集成 1Q26 部分製程：10% 2Q26 2Q26
2026年兩岸晶圓代工業者漲價概況
• 由於地緣政治動盪、原物料成本上升影響，半導體供應鏈普遍面
臨生產成本提升的壓力。
• DIGITIMES觀察，兩岸重點晶圓代工業者已於2025年第3季起，陸

續宣告調漲代工價格，反映特定製程需求火熱。
• 台積電率先於2025年第3季宣布先進製程價格調升3~10%，並於
2026年初生效，反映AI/HPC需求是支撐先進製程需求的重要動能，
也反映先進製程供給依舊吃緊。
• AI帶動基型記憶體需求緊俏，力積電也於2026年第1季宣布大幅調
漲記憶體代工價格。
• DIGITIMES觀察，2026年晶圓代工漲價已不再侷限於先進製程，包
含8吋與部分12吋成熟製程也同步調升代工價格，反映整體供需結
構已改變。
• 台積電、三星電子(Samsung Electronics)推動關閉8吋製程產能計
畫，推動半導體供應鏈開始尋找替代供應商，也帶動聯電、世界
先進、力積電等業者漲價。
• 中國晶圓代工廠包括中芯國際、華虹集團與晶合集成也陸續於
8

### Page 11

2025年第4季至2026年第1季宣布漲價，除反映中國當地需求回升
外，也隱含中國成熟製程市場逐漸從過去價格競爭，轉向獲利與
產能利用率平衡策略，並且進一步改善毛利率。
• 兩岸晶圓代工業者漲價效應已自2026年第1季底開始陸續發酵，聯
電漲價則在2026年第3季生效，對營收挹注時點較晚。
8

1Q25~2Q26台灣與中國晶圓代工營收變化與預估

台廠 中企
漲價效應發酵 2Q26 兩岸晶圓代工營收將季增
單位：十億美元
• DIGITIMES預估，2026年第2季台灣晶圓代工業者在HPC與AI基礎設施需求持
續增加，以及漲價因素發酵的背景下，合計營收將季增逾10%，年增估達3成。
• 5/3奈米等先進製程節點營收貢獻佔比將持續攀升，生成式AI從雲端訓練轉向
訓練與推論，使HPC晶片需求不侷限於AI伺服器，也帶動通用型伺服器需求
擴增。
• 成熟製程雖受消費性電子晶片需求疲軟影響，但晶片需求轉向車載娛樂系統、
無人機等應用，使台廠晶圓代工訂單暫未明顯受影響。
• 台廠漲價效應將於第2季起發酵，帶動營收季增。
• 中系晶圓代工2026年第2季營收仍將維持季增，消費政策補貼、漲價成為支撐
產業成長動能，第2季營收將季增逾10％，年增估逾20%。
• 中系晶圓代工業者缺乏AI相關成長動能，主要應用聚焦車用、工控、消費性
電子，除車用工控需求相對穩定，消費性電子需求則依靠購新補貼政策帶動。
• 中芯國際雖跨足14奈米以下先進製程，但其主要營收來源仍以28奈米以上成

熟製程為主。
• 中芯國際、華虹集團有部分利基型記憶體代工業務，也渴望提供營收動能。
• 中系業者已於2025年第4季至2026年第1季宣布漲價，漲價效應將在第2季浮
現，也成為帶動營收成長的主要動能。
• 2025年上半中系業者採用低價競爭策略，比較基期較低，使2026年第2季中
國晶圓代工營收將年增近3成。
9

• 綜觀兩岸業者各家技術策略，台積電持續領先並挺進埃米世代，
聯電穩守成熟製程並尋求技術升級，而中芯國際與華虹半導體則
在美國制裁壓力下，力求製程突破。
• 台積電2026年製程發展重點在於量產2奈米性能增強版N2P，同時
N3系列製程(包含N3P、N3X、N3A)將繼續擴張，以覆蓋HPC與車

用電子需求。
• 台積電2027~2029年將朝A16、A14、A13、A12等埃米製程開發。
• 台積電採用奈米片架構與背面供電技術，維持製程性能提升。
• 聯電則持續深耕28/22/14奈米製程，並與英特爾(Intel)合作開發12
奈米製程，同時也將探索更先進製程的技術路徑。
• 2027年將推出12奈米製程，為聯電與英特爾合作下的技術產出，
提供客戶更具性價比的FinFET選項。
• 雙方除合作開發12奈米製程，也進一步探索更先進製程的合作路
徑。
• 2027年將推出由比利時研究機構IMEC授權的12吋矽光子製程PDK
1.0套件，並提供混合鍵合(hybrid bonding)、矽穿孔(TSV)技術及調

• 中系業者即使面臨美國半導體出口管制措施，仍加強半導體自主

兩岸主要晶圓代工業者先進製程布局及產能變化
• 台積電2026年除持續加速N3/2產能擴張，也持續推動成熟製程轉型計畫，將
調整Fab 15A及Fab 15B產能，但目標期程可能受制人力短缺而有不確定性。
• Fab 15A P1將進行製程改造，將原先N28/22製程設備挪往德國ESMC，並將導
入新設備生產N4製程；另外部分製程則轉向生產矽中介層。
• Fab 15B P7將既有N7/6製程轉為N3，滿足3奈米製程的強勁需求。
• 考量台積電多座新廠進行N3/2製程擴產，包含工程工班、設備商支援工程師
等人力已相當緊缺，恐影響產線設備調整、二次配、進機等時程規劃，導致
目前設置的目標可能受影響。
• 中系晶圓代工業者將持續擴展先進製程發展。
• 中芯國際持續在中芯南方推動N+3製程開發及產能提升。

• 華虹集團則將在旗下子公司華力微的華虹六廠開發7奈米製程產能。
• 原規劃月產能2萬片，但受美國制裁影響，有效產能恐將不如原預期。

2024~2026年兩岸主要晶圓代工業者資本支出變化及預估
2024 2025 2026(e)
2026 年兩岸晶圓代工業者僅台積電資本支出大幅增加
資料來源：各業者，DIGITIMES整理，2026/5
單位：十億美元
• 2024~2026年兩岸主要晶圓代工業者的資本支出呈現兩極化的發展
格局，先進製程業者持續增加資本支出推動技術發展，成熟製程
業者則以較保守的策略持續優化產品組合提高毛利。
• 台積電為鞏固先進製程的領先地位及先進封裝的布局，資本支出
已從2024年約291億美元，至2026年，將投入540億美元，幾乎翻

倍成長。
• 中芯為求供應鏈自主，持續維持近80億美元的資本支出，力求掌
握7奈米以下先進製程生產能力。
• 聯電、世界先進與華虹等成熟製程業者則採取較保守的策略，資
本支出將維持在20億美元以下。
• 聯電2026年以提升新加坡Fab12i P3特殊製程產能為主，資本支出
預估將呈現持平至小幅年減。
• 華虹集團雖有華虹九廠特殊製程產能提升、興建華虹成都、建置7
奈米製程產能等規畫，但2026年資本支出將年減。

2021~2026年兩岸晶圓代工業營收變化與預估
台灣 中國
台灣YoY 中國YoY單位：十億美元
• DIGITIMES預估，2026年兩岸晶圓代工業營收皆將呈現超過20%的高成長動能，
調漲晶圓代工價格是雙邊共同的營收成長驅動力，而台灣主要依靠AI應用帶
動，中國則持續仰賴政策支持。
• DIGITIMES預估，2026年台灣晶圓代工業合計營收將年增超過3成，上看1,770

億美元，調漲代工價格及AI/HPC應用需求強勁為營收成長主因。
• 扣除台積電的台灣晶圓代工業者合計營收將年增約18%，達126億美元。
• DIGITIMES預估，2026年中國晶圓代工業合計營收將達190億美元，年增估達
25%，除持續受惠政策與內需市場，漲價也挹注營收成長。
• 中國半導體自主化政策、購新補貼政策將持續帶動中系業者訂單需求。
• 兩岸晶圓代工業者雖依靠漲價帶動營收成長，但同樣也面對消費性電子需求
疲軟的壓力。
• 記憶體價格仍將是影響2026年消費性電子需求的重點因素。
• 消費性電子晶片訂單部分移轉到車用、無人機等應用晶片，部分則加強轉向
利基型記憶體，暫未見大舉砍單跡象。
• 扣除漲價因素，2026年台灣晶圓代工業者持續依靠AI主晶片或週邊晶片，以
及部分尋求「China+1」的供應要求，維持營運成長動能；然而，中國晶圓代
工業者持續開出新產能及漲價，台廠成熟製程業者壓力有增無減。

### Page 20

16 玉山證券投資顧問(股)公司 dannywen0523-72306@esunsec.com.tw 於 26/05/22 07:37 下載
結語：AI與漲價因素帶動 2Q26 兩岸晶圓代工產業營
收動能可期 亦將提升2026 全年營收增幅
1Q26、2Q26及2026全年兩岸晶圓代工業發展重點議題彙整
資料來源：DIGITIMES，2026/5
• 淡季不淡，1Q26兩岸晶圓代工業營收皆呈現個位數季增，但成因不同。
• AI應用是推升台灣晶圓代工季營收成長主因，中系業者則受惠消費性政策補貼。
1Q26兩岸晶圓代工業發展概況
• 2Q26受惠漲價因素帶動，兩岸的晶圓代工產業營收皆將季增逾10%。
• 2026全年受惠漲價及AI需求強勁等因素，兩岸產業營收皆將年增25%以上。
• 消費性電子晶片訂單移轉至其他應用，兩岸晶圓代工業暫未見明顯砍單。
2Q26及2026全年兩岸產業發展重點議題
• DIGITIMES觀察，2026年兩岸晶圓代工業者合計營收皆呈現季增，反映淡季
不淡的市場發展，但雙邊成因並不同。

• 2026年第1季台廠合計營收季增6%，主要受台積電營收拉動，若扣除台積電，
台廠合計營收則略下滑約1%，但仍優於傳統淡季效應的幅度。
• 2026年第1季中國晶圓代工營收季增1.7%，主要受惠中國消費補貼政策拉動
需求。
• 展望2026年第2季，受惠漲價效應發酵，兩岸晶圓代工業營收皆將呈現超過
10%增幅，也將挹注全年營收成長動能。
• AI仍是支撐兩岸晶圓代工業營收的重要應用。
• 消費性電子需求雖受記憶體價格飆漲影響，但相關晶片訂單已逐漸移轉到車
用、工控、無人機等應用，或轉向利基型記憶體，兩岸晶圓代工業者尚未見
到明顯砍單跡象。
• 晶圓代工業者漲價效應於2026年第1季底陸續發酵，將推升第2季及全年營收
成長幅度。
• DIGITIMES預估，2026年第2季兩岸的晶圓代工營收皆將季增逾10％。
• 2026年台灣晶圓代工業合計營收將年增超過3成，達1,770億美元；中國晶圓
代工業全年合計營收將年增估達25%，達190億美元。
16

主要從事精密滾珠螺桿、線性傳動元件與工業型機械人研發製造，以自有品牌

注約 1.03 億元，稅後 EPS 1.64 元，優於預期之 1.06 元。

2Q26 營運展望正向 ， 訂單能見度
上看 4.5-5 個月，預估稅後 EPS 
2.11 元

2Q26 營運展望正向，訂單能見度上看 4.5-5 個月，預估稅後 EPS 2.11 元 
上銀 4 月營收為 24.7 億元，+5.0%MoM，+20.7%Y oY，營收達成率 34%，
優於預期，係因線軌與滾珠螺桿兩大產品線溫和復甦。展望後市，目前訂單能
見度滾珠螺桿 4.5-5 個月 、 線性滑軌3.5 個月 ， 農歷年至今都在加班去化訂單 ，
公司表示接下來幾個月的營收獲利不會低於 3-4 月 。 此外 ， 管理層表示反應原
物料上漲 ，3 月有調漲售價 ， 不同產品 、 不同區域漲幅不同 ， 平均漲幅10-15%。
考量產品交期長達 3-4 個月， 預計漲價效益在2Q26 季底才會顯現， 故元富調
整 2Q26 營收至 71.7 億元(原 61.3 億元)，+12.4%QoQ，+21%Y oY。獲利部
分，在產能利用率上升，且費用支出較為固定下，對毛利看法正向，不考慮一
次性業外損益下，調整稅後 EPS 至 2.11 元(原 0.96 元)。

產業谷底復甦 ， 調整2026 年稅後
EPS 至 8.49 元 
 產業谷底復甦，調整 2026 年稅後 EPS 至 8.49 元 
3M26 日本工具機訂單達 1,935 億日圓，+31.9%MoM，+28.1%Y oY，累積
+26%Y oY，美國市場受惠半導體與航太需求帶動累計+48.3%Y oY，中國市場
受惠於汽車與自動化需求，累計 +46.2%Y oY，歐洲市場也重回成長軌道，累
計+19.9%Y oY，2026 年工具機市場已進入景氣回升階段。展望後市，公司對
2026 年各地區看法 : 美國回升力道不錯，川普美國製造效應發酵，台灣工具
機銷往美國有很多機會，歐洲汽車動能回復仍不佳，自動化需求有工業 4.0 的
持續推升表現相對穩定，中國市場則因機器人、半導體、 AI 相關需求很熱，
來自中國經銷反饋，認為中國工具機市場到年底都很熱；機器人部門為 2026
年成長主軸 ， 主要以1)半導體設備(晶圓機器人何晶圓移載系統)需求隨半導體
客戶設廠挹注營運動能；2)美系物流機器人：1H26 客戶小量投入生產線，初
步金額貢獻有限，最快 2H26 會逐步增加出貨，總結前述，調整 2026 年營收
至 285.8 億元(原 259.2 億元)，+8.5%Y oY。獲利部分，受惠訂單回籠、產能
利用率將逐步回升，加計下半 年漲價效應，價量齊揚下有望推升獲利逐季走
揚，故調整 2026 年稅 EPS 至 8.49 元(原 5.58 元)。

營運展望正向，惟評價合理，維
持中立評等，目標價 350 元 
 營運展望正向，惟評價合理，維持中立評等，目標價 350 元 
管理層對 2026 年營運展望正向，且 AI 資料中心的興起帶動技術革新，新製程
採用帶動眾多電子行業的擴廠動作、設備採購，設備零件、次系統或自動化配
套需求明顯增加。過往評價區間處 18-40x 水準，當前公司評價處 42x，評價偏
高，故維持中立評等，目標價 350 元(41x2026 EPS)。

Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
ABF Substrates – Takeaways
from AT&S FY25/26 Results Call
AT&S raised revenue and EBITDA margin guidance for FY26/27,
which supports our bullish view on ABF substrate suppliers
Unimicron, NYPCB, and ZDT.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Howard Kao
Equity Analyst
Howard.Kao@morganstanley.com                                               +886 2 2730-2989
Irene Yen
Research Associate
Irene.Yen@morganstanley.com                                               +886 2 2730-2869

Sharon Shih
Equity Analyst
Sharon.Shih@morganstanley.com                                               +886 2 2730-2865
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Medium-term 2026/27 guidance
• Revenue: +30-35% y/y (implying the high end of the communicated €2.1-
2.4bn range)
• EBITDA margin: 25-29% (raised from 24-28% previously)
• *This guidance is subject to supply and the geopolitical situation, as well as
currency developments. Also not included are the potential effects of

ongoing negotiations with customers. These would result in a capacity
expansion that is partially financed by customers and would accordingly
impact earnings performance and capital expenditure planning.
Other key takeaways:
• (+) FY26/27 revenue growth will be mainly driven by volume growth, with
Microelectronics (ABF substrate) seeing more pronounced growth from AI
infra investments, which impact demand for both GPU and increasingly CPU
as the ecosystem broadens towards more inference workloads.

• (+) AT&S noted that customer demand for its technologies is so large that
some of them cannot be fulfilled; particularly for Microelectronics, it is
seeing strong demand from both existing and new customers.
• (+) Now AT&S' agreements with its customers often include financing for
investments from its customers, one example being the newly announced
capacity expansion for high-end substrates in Chongqing, China, for which the
required investments in the 'high double-digit € million' range will be fully

financed based on long-term customer agreements. This is expected to
ensure profitability for AT&S.
• (+) AT&S also noted increasing input costs from suppliers, which AT&S
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
AT&S (ATSV.VI, not covered) reported C1Q revenue of €477mn (+2% q/q):
Electronics Solutions (-13% q/q) and Microelectronics (ABF; +14% q/q).
C1Q/F4Q Microelectronics (ABF) EBIT margin jumped by 920bp q/q to 17.4%,
which management attributed to bigger volumes in all sites and pricing effects.
FY26/27 revenue was guided to grow 30-35% y/y, primarily driven by volumes,

with Microelectronics (ABF) seeing more pronounced growth...
...and EBITDA margin was guided to fall in the range of 25-29% (vs. 23.3% in
FY25/26), on the back of volumes, pricing and passing on increasing input costs.
May 21, 2026 04:11 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
Update
2
expects to pass on to its customers, though typically with a delay of a few
months. However, management is confident that it will be able to balance
cost increases and price hikes for the remainder of this year.
• (+) AT&S confirmed that it did adjust the pricing for its ABF substrate
products in C1Q/F4Q.
• (=)  AT&S is also engaging with partners and customers to develop future
technologies, including larger substrates, glass core substrates, embedded

component substrates/PCBs, optical PCBs, etc.
Our thoughts: Management sounded very positive during the call, citing several
growth tailwinds for ABF substrates, including larger volumes, more customer
commitments and continuing pricing actions to offset inflationary cost pressures.
This echoes our view that we are just in the early phase of an upcycle, and that the
substrate suppliers will be able to expand margins through pricing adjustments that

more than offset the cost pressures. We believe AT&S' view on ABF substrates
aligns with our positive view on Unimicron, NYPCB, and Zhen Ding.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers
n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn&#39;t require substrate
(e.g., CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Zhen Ding (4958.TW)

Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 10%, a medium-
term growth rate of 15% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Higher-than-expected F-PCB content increase for iPhones
n Better-than-expected production yield/share allocation for SLP
n Share gains on higher margin F-PCB pieces
Risks to Downside
n Worse-than-expected iPhone sell-through
n Lower-than-expected F-PCB content increase for iPhones

n Worse-than-expected production yield/share allocation for SLP
n Increasing competition from Chinese peers, intensifying pricing pressure
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand
n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 065: 260522_ubs_TW-PCB-substrate

Source PDF: all report pdf/260522_ubs_TW-PCB-substrate.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-05-22
Pages: 42
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 22 May 2026
Global Research
Taiwan PCB/Substrates
Still at the early innings of a multi-year upcycle
Tailwinds for substrates to strengthen further
We lift estimates for all three substrate makers to factor in accelerated growth from
capacity expansion and stronger than expected pricing amid industry undersupply
dynamic with meaningful uplift in margins; and raise PTs to factor in more margin upside
and earnings power expansion.  Substrates stocks have rallied on accelerated AI demand

that are driving pricing and margins to move up, but has taken a breather post Q1 results
and likely factored in concerns on oversupply from capacity expansions. We note that
most of the major substrate companies are looking to expand capacity by only 20-50%
in the coming years, suggesting further upside risk to pricing has yet to be priced in. We
believe there's still more upside to come  in a structurally different cycle driven by AI
which we anticipate much more favourable pricing among imbalance supply/demand

and valuation should drift higher as margins/earnings start to manifest. We lift our PTs
for Unimicron/Kinsus/NYPCB by 94%/70%/52% on earnings upward revisions and
moving from a P/BV-based approach to a P/E-based methodology, reflecting improving
earnings visibility and higher ROE that are expected to be stronger for longer.
Unimicron (Buy, lift PT to NT$1200): We continue to  like Unimicron as the clear
technology leader as key suppliers across NVDA GPU and major ASIC platforms. We now

forecast '26/'27/'28 sales to grow 37%/38%/25% YoY (up 4%/13%/13% from prior
sales estimates) with momentum to accelerate from H226E as a number of AI GPU/ASIC
project start to ramp with potential upside from AI CPUs and general server. We
anticipate high UTR and pricing uplifts will lead to 30-35% GM for ABF in H226 and
further improve to 35-40% level in 2027E. This will lead to 2026/2027/2028E GM of
21%/29%/32%, up from our prior forecasts of 20%/25%/26%. HDI margins should

gradually tick up on improving yield rates; while BT margins likely remain below
corporate average as the company adjust its production lines.
Kinsus (Buy, lift PT to NT$680): We reiterate Buy and see rising appeal on better
growth outlook from AI related projects, notably in CPUs. We now forecast '26/'27/'28
sales to grow 31%/37%/31%% YoY (up 4%/19%/30% from prior sales estimates)  as
we anticipate stronger growth from pricing uplifts and ABF capacity additions of +22%/

+20% in '26/'27E (was +11%/+10% in '26/'27E), which will remain fully loaded as it
gains share in NVDA CPU substrate while ramping ASICs and general server CPU
substrate. We also noted  it is looking to solidify customer relationship through strategic
investments, which could potentially  serve as the next catalyst.
NYPCB (Neutral, lift PT to NT$875):  The company enjoys stronger pricing power given
more orders based on spot prices that allows it to enjoy the full benefit of price hikes

sooner than peers. Nonetheless, we anticipate limited ABF capacity additions until 2028,
which could lead to slower-than-peers volume growth despite  favourable pricing.  We
now forecast '26/'27/'28 sales to grow 40%/44%/26% YoY (up 6%/18%/39% from
prior sales estimates) with GM moving toward 20%/26%/29% on  loaded capacity and
favourable pricing/sourcing, up from our prior forecasts of 18%/24%/24%.

Q: Could ABF drive the next phase of growth for Unimicron?
Yes. Among Taiwanese suppliers, we believe Unimicron is best positioned to capitalise on rapid AI
deployment based on its comprehensive offerings across GPU, CPU and ASIC AI servers. We expect
Unimicron to gain share in Rubin GPUs, and will continue to benefit from growing AI server TAM as a

key supplier for several US cloud service providers' AI ASIC substrates. We flag other drivers including
size increases and content gains for general servers and switch when migrating to future generations.
We are positive on the outlook and see higher utilization rate, more substrate pricing uplifts,
improving yield rate could drive margin expansion in 2026E/2027E/2028E.
Q: Will the non-ABF substrate business grow?
Moderately with upside potential from HDI ramp, in our view. The company has not aggressively

expanded its RPCB (Rigid PCB) capacity, but chose to optimise HDI production as it shifts focus from
smartphones to AI servers. We believe Unimicron was the dominant high-density interconnect (HDI)
supplier of Hopper OAM boards although it has lost some share on the Blackwell to Victory Giant.
That being said, we are starting to see yield rate improvement for its Taiwan HDI fab, and anticipate
volume for this fab would start to ramp from Q226, driving more growth in 2026E/2027E.

We maintain a Buy rating as we continue to expect Unimicron to deliver above-industry sales growth
in 2025-29E, as we view it well positioned for  structural tailwinds from AI servers, general servers and
CPO deployment. We believe it is set for strong growth as a major substrate supplier for Nvidia GPU
and multiple ASIC substrates, its HDI business is finally gaining momentum, and  also it is involved in a
number of US CSP ASIC projects. We model sales to grow 37%/38%/25% in 2026/2027/2028E, led

by ABF substrates.
A number of  positive drivers for ABF substrate growth including: 1) AI servers: substantial volume
growth and content gains in new platforms , eg  at least 50-100% increase in area for ABF substrate
consumption from Hopper to Blackwell, and up another 50-100% from Blackwell to Rubin; 2)
general servers: body size increase and higher layer count to drive 20-50% content gains when
migrating to new platforms; 3) networking/switch1.6T/800G substrate capacity consumption

doubling from the 800G/400G migrations.
Unimicron is trading at 30x/19x  2027/2028E P/E, compared to Taiwan hardware/components peers
trading average at 33x/26x.  We believe it  has priced in the substrate price increases to date driven by
mostly material price hikes and some pricing premium; yet considering the industry is entering a
structurally different upcycle from AI server fundamentals with more margin upside  on the way, we
believe the stock has not fully factored in  the stronger pricing bargaining power supported by AI-

driven demand upside and stronger-than-expected margin expansion.

Taiwan PCB/Substrates22 May 2026 ab 5
Taiwan PCB/Substrates UBS Research
Substrate in full-fledged undersupply
The substrate industry has entered a period of acute shortages driven by: 1) strong  ABF
demand driven by AI requirement for higher layer count and larger substrate sizes,
which is also lifting the demand for switch that also requires substrates; 2) a robust
conventional server refresh cycle that are calling for substantial needs of CPUs; 3)

capacity additions from key components such as t-glass are much slower than demand
expansion.
Low-CTE supply constraints to carry on for longer
AI demand acceleration is driving material upgrades that are essential  for high-speed,
high frequency, and high reliability computing performance, where T-glass comes in to
act as a critical material to help hold up multi-layer substrates sharp without warping
while offering low dielectric properties and low thermal expansion, etc.

Nittobo (3110.T, 70%+ share in global T-glass market):
 The company has been facing low CTE glass capacity shortages, whose output is
1) mostly allocated to Resonac  that sells mainly to Ibiden and Unimicron for large
body-size FCBGA substrates (UBSe: Resonac has 60-70% share in ABF substrates);
2) smaller allocation to MGC that serves high-end FCCSP and memory substrate
applications sold by chipset and memory makers.
 In August 2025, BoD approved ¥15 billion investments  to triple its capacity at its

Fukushima plant, with new production slated to start in FQ426 (ending Mar '27)
 The company currently has around 500K (m2) low CTE glass fabric capacity. Under
its medium-term management plan, Nittobo is investing $530M+ total in T-glass
over four years, looking to triple t-glass capacity by 2028 vs 2025 with Taiwan
capacity potentially doubling by Q128.
 Baotek Industrial Materials (5340.TW) is a subsidiary of Nittobo Group (47%
holding) that specializes in high-end fiberglass fabrics, including T-glass, which

technology support from its mother group. The company noted high-end products
has exceeded 50% of total sales in H225 and anticipates t-glass volume will start
to ramp from Q226.
Taiwan Glass (1802.TW)
 Taiwan Glass is the 2nd largest high-end glass clothes supplier. It currently has
roughly 100K (m2) low CTE glass fabric capacity in Taiwan that are fully loaded.
 The company has previously noted that is low dk 1/dk2 and low CTE products have
all received customer qualifications and is looking to expand its high-end glass

clothes supply (not limited to low CTE, e.g. t-glass, but also low DK) by 40-50% by
2027 with good order visibility.
Nan Ya Plastics (1303.TW)
 Our industry checks suggest Nan Ya Plastics  qualified for their low CTE products in
late 2025, but their progress in winning AI projects remain relatively slow. Glass
clothes currently only contributes 2-3% of Nan Ya Plastic total sales (or 5-6% of its
electronic materials sales, which accounts for roughly 50% of total sales).

 Nan Ya Plastics is partnering with Nittobo to weave 20% of the latter's glass
clothes supply by 2027. Beyond the collaboration on production, Nittobo will also
provide stable supply on key raw materials for low-dielectric-constant glass yarn
(NRE).
Other companies include Taishan Glass with 40-50K (m2) low CTE glass fabric capacity
in China, who is looking to double that capacity by end of 2026; and Fulltech (1815.TW)
who we believe have small volume production in Taiwan and also looking to expand in

Thailand. The supply chain have been looking for alternatives, our studies suggest
Taiwan Glass is making more progress on projects win, while Nan Ya Plastics has also
received qualification with a number of customers. That being said, the yield rate and
feedback is still climbing the learning curve where the actual supporting volume
delivered to customers remains insufficient. We expect supply will remain constrained
through 2026/2027, and even into 2028.

Preparing for more structural demand growth
We reiterated our view (link) that this cycle is structurally different than the prior upcycle
during 2019-2022, which were primarily driven by the sudden surge  in PC demand
where a lot of capacity expansion was then set in motion and substrate makers had
come to agreement with their customers to facilitate capacity expansion. Unfortunately,
consumer demand fell short of expectations coming out of the pandemic that led to a

sharp decline in substrate market pricing amid oversupply in 2023-2024. The substrate
market troughed in early 2024 and slowly recovered, supported by general computing
demand and moderate PC recovery, later followed by AI taking off with strength
continuing till today. We believe AI is driving a different upcycle for substrate makers
that will be stronger for longer as not only unit growth but also requirement for larger
substrate size and higher layer count will serve as strong demand tailwind.

GPU/ASICs  has been in the spotlight, driving AI supply chain momentum.
UBS highlighted the major hyperscalers spending and monetization trends provide
updated signals that AI momentum should continue - where now estimates for the top 5
hyperscalers capex in 2026-27E will grow by +61%/+14% YoY to US$719/819bn (
link).
UBS analyst Tim Arcuri projects NVDA shipment of 5.7MM GPUs in C2025 and a step up
to ~9.0MM/9.9MM/9.5MM in C2026/2027/2028 (+57%/+10%/-4% YoY). Besides the

substantial growth witnessed in GPU/ASIC volumes and breadth, Agentic AI is driving a
step-function increase in the importance of CPUs to perform workload orchestration.
UBS noted (
Link) that agentic deployments generally require a ~3–5x increase in CPU
cores per user and per GPU/XPU, and anticipate the server CPU TAM could grow ~5x
through C2030E from ~$30B in C2025 to ~$170B in C2030E.

On top of the unit economies driven by GPU/ASICs and CPU demand, we continue to
see content uplift in new platforms. From Nvidia's Hopper to Blackwell generation, we
see substrate area consumption goes up by 115%, and another +70-85% uplift from
Blackwell to Rubin, as substrate layer counts look to advance further with increasing
computing dies and HBM stack counts. Besides Nvidia's AI GPU roadmap, we also
anticipate continued advancement in ASICs that will require larger and more complex

substrates. At the meantime, we are also seeing body size and high layer count been
driving 20-50% content gains in general servers; while networking/switch1.6T/800G
substrate capacity consumption are looking to double from the 800G/400G migrations.
We believe larger packaging size and higher layer count will drive ABF substrate demand
to outgrow end market unit growth.

Capacity expansion plans backed by LTAs and potential private
placement
Although more companies have been announcing their expansion plans, we continue to
believe that this expansion would not alter the near-term supply shortage gap. Due to
the long equipment lead time and time required to fully set up the proper infrastructure
and ramp production yields, we expect any greenfield expansion would require at least

1.5 to 2 years in total to finish the entire process of building construction, equipment
pull-in, test run and qualification.

Pricing upside would not be capped by LTAs and capacity expansion
Some view the capacity announcements mentioned above as a negative signal for
substrate industry. Nonetheless, we believe these announcements speaks to the fact

that high-end ABF demand growth is expanding so fast that all players are looking to
catch up on customer demand. Our prior section has talked about multiple secular
drivers across GPU, ASICs, networking/switch, general servers, and agentic AI, etc. AI
opportunities have risen beyond the industry's demand expectations for the next few
years, where the capacity expansion in place were insufficient to meet customers
forecasts.
We have started to see substrate makers lifting ABF and BT substrate pricing in H225,

where the initial price increases are primarily to reflect higher material costs including T-
glass, copper, CCL, etc; which has started to gradually reflect in the revenue numbers.
Our studies suggest the entire supply chain are now even more eager to secure capacity
thus the pricing negotiation frequency has increased not just from the suppliers end but
also on the end customers side, who are willing to take on the premium on intensified
supply bottleneck. In May 2026, Ajinomoto has informed IC substrate makers that it will

lift ABF film prices by 30%, effective from Q326. The company holds 80%+ share in the
ABF laminated film material, further escalating costs for IC substrate supply chain.
Similar to prior upcycle, long-term agreements (LTAs) for dedicated capacity are signed
during this cycle. What is different from last time, we are seeing more prepayment
upfront with pricing incentives to ramp production.  In addition, the counter parties for
LTAs are with major CSPs and chipset vendors who are dedicated to push through AI

servers system upgrades and infrastructure buildouts. While none of the terms have
been disclosed, we believe substrate makers have signed or are looking to sign LTAs or
potential collaboration model to help reduce business risks. We believe Unimicron has
multiple LTAs signed considering its technology leadership and serving as major supplier
across  handful of key projects for GPUs (NVDA) and ASICs (Google, AWS) where
customers are looking to secure more volumes. Other suppliers are also looking into co-

kevinlu@ lenovo.comkevinlu@ lenovo.com

### Page 10

Taiwan PCB/Substrates22 May 2026 ab 10
investment model such as private placement with their customers to reduce risk from
customers walking away from the LTAs without penalty while ensuring orders will help
maintain a certain level of utilization.
We note that the LTAs in place do not lock in pricing, and we anticipate the price hikes in
key materials will push up production costs and gradually pass through to customers.
We expect ABF and BT substrate pricing would continue to rise until the end of the year,

with more significant adjustments in the H226. We now forecast ABF substrate pricing
would be up 15-20% YoY in 2026, and will further accelerate into 2027 as supply/
demand gap will likely widen.
Thoughts on technologies under development
1) Glass substrates
Compared to organic substrates, glass materials offer superior dimensional stability,
thermal conductivity, and electrical properties. For starters, glass core substrates
leverage TGV (Through Glass Via) technology for via formation that allows smaller via

sizes and higher density interconnects relative to the mechanical drilling used for ABF
substrates. In addition, traditional ABF substrates require additional warpage treatment
during layer build-up processing where glass substrate's flat surface allows more precise
lithography and multi-layer alignment.
Glass core substrates offer better performance and scalability for next-generation
chipsets and packaging requirements that have been driven by applications and

workloads requiring larger form factor packages, including high performance
computing, data centers, AI, graphics, higher speed capabilities, etc. The core layer of
ABF substrates, ie CCL, currently is dominated by Japanese supplier Resonac, which is
actively researching other materials in case of potentially being replaced by glass
manufacturers such as Corning, Schott, etc; while substrate makers are also exploring
potential alternatives other than glass.

Figure 8: Next-gen glass core substrate overview
Source: Company data, UBS
 Timing and implications for Taiwanese PCB/substrate makers:  Unimicron
noted that it is still closely working with its customers on glass materials studies
and researching manufacturing processes based on glass properties. The company
has been working on glass substrate engineering samples and qualification since
2025, but management expects volume production will not take place until

2027/28. Yield rate remains challenging as several issues remain to be resolved.
During the glass drilling process, laser or chemical etching still face problems such
as inconsistent hole shape and high surface roughness; and the risk of uneven
filling may lead to insulation degradation and long-term failure. Ibiden's
technology roadmap suggests Glass substrates would unlikely be widely adopted
kevinlu@ lenovo.comkevinlu@ lenovo.com

### Page 11

Taiwan PCB/Substrates22 May 2026 ab 11
until 2030; while SEMCO is operating a pilot production line with Japan's
Sumitomo Chemical Group to secure glass core material supply with mass
production likely taking place after 2027.
2) CoWoP (Chip-On-Wafer-on-PCB)
CoWoP (Chip-on-Wafer-on-PCB) is an NVIDIA-led advanced packaging architecture,
developed with partners such as TSMC, that targets lower cost and improved scalability
versus CoWoS by eliminating the ABF substrate layer and directly bonding the silicon

interposer plus GPU/HBM assembly onto a large platform PCB. This substrate-less design
leverages mature PCB manufacturing to support high-speed NVLink interconnects and
HBM integration while enabling larger form factors for AI systems. Technically, CoWoP
retains a silicon interposer with TSVs and fine-pitch RDL for high-density interconnects,
with chip integration via micro-bump or hybrid bonding, but shifts system-level routing
to advanced PCB technologies such as HDI and mSAP (typically ~20–35 μm line/space).

This enables shorter interconnect paths, improving signal and power integrity, while the
lidless design supports more efficient thermal dissipation.
The primary advantage of CoWoP lies in cost and scalability. By removing the ABF
substrate and simplifying the packaging stack, it can deliver an estimated 30–50%
reduction in packaging cost while enabling panel-level manufacturing (e.g., ~450 × 450
mm), which improves throughput and capacity ramp timelines. However, the trade-off

lies in lower routing precision versus CoWoS and increased manufacturing challenges,
including PCB warpage, CTE mismatch, and the need for substrate-like precision in PCB
fabrication. As such, CoWoP is expected to complement rather than replace CoWoS,
which remains the preferred solution for highest-performance AI GPUs.
Figure 9: CoWoS to CoWoP: removal of package lid/substrate and BGA balls
Source: Company data, UBS
 Timing and implications for Taiwanese PCB/substrate makers: Taiwanese

companies noted that CoWoP remains at experimental stage as major technical
hurdles persist. Achieving substrate-like performance would require PCB line/
space to shrink from ~20/35 μm to sub-10 μm, well beyond current
manufacturing capabilities, creating challenges in yield, warpage, and reliability.
Our industry checks suggests Unimicron and SEMCO are working on this
technology as customers have not cancelled or completely moved away from
CoWoP; but has limited visibility on commercialization timing, and other substrate

makers such as Kinsus and NYPCB has not mentioned much on CoWoP
investments.
3) EMIB-T (Embedded Multi-die Interconnect Bridge-Through-Silicon
Via)
Embedded Multi-die Interconnect Bridge (EMIB) is Intel’s 2.5D advanced packaging
technology designed to support high-performance applications such as AI, machine
learning (ML), and high-performance computing (HPC), where high data throughput
and low latency are critical. Unlike CoWoS, which relies on a large silicon interposer,

EMIB embeds small silicon bridges directly into an organic ABF substrate, enabling
localized high-density interconnects while maintaining a simpler and more cost-efficient
structure. This hybrid approach allows heterogeneous integration with tight microbump
kevinlu@ lenovo.comkevinlu@ lenovo.com

### Page 12

Taiwan PCB/Substrates22 May 2026 ab 12
pitch only at the bridge and relaxed pitch elsewhere, improving scalability, design
flexibility, and yield while avoiding the cost and size limitations of full interposers. EMIB
has been in high-volume manufacturing since 2017 and is widely deployed across server,
networking, and HPC applications.
EMIB-T builds upon the proven technology of EMIB by incorporating TSVs to enhance
package power delivery efficiency, ultra-high-speed UCIe die-to-die communications to

enable HBM integration on package. The shift toward EMIB-T is driven by two structural
factors. First, CoWoS capacity constraints, largely tied up by NVIDIA GPUs, limit access
for other hyperscalers. Second, EMIB-T offers cost and size advantages, as it removes the
need for large interposers and reduces manufacturing complexity. However, EMIB
involves trade-offs: while it is more cost-efficient and scalable, it typically has higher
latency and lower interconnect density than CoWoS, making it better suited for ASIC

and less interconnect-intensive designs, whereas CoWoS remains preferred for
maximum-performance AI GPUs.
Figure 10:  EMIB-T acting as small silicon "bridges"
Source: Intel
Figure 11: Incorporating TSVs to enhance performance
Source: Intel
From a supply chain perspective, EMIB-T is structurally supportive of the ABF ecosystem,
as it continues to rely on organic substrates rather than replacing them. The ecosystem
includes ABF film and leading substrate manufacturers such as Unimicron and Ibiden,

which benefit from sustained demand for advanced substrate capacity. This contrasts
with CoWoP , which removes the substrate layer entirely. As a result, EMIB-T represents a
stabilizing factor for ABF demand, particularly as AI ASIC development accelerates
among hyperscalers. That being said, there are manufacturing and engineering hurdles
now that ABF substrates are taking on the routing and power delivery. It calls for  ultra-
high-density ABF substrates with stringent requirements for finer line/space  routing,

advanced laser via drilling, die warpage, and embedded cavity precision, etc. 3D
stacking allows SoIC to deliver better performance but at the expense of thermal
constraints and manufacturing complexity.
Looking ahead, EMIB-T adoption is expected to ramp gradually, with design cycles of
approximately 18 months and production cycles of ~12 months, implying meaningful
revenue contribution likely around 2028. Initial deployments are expected to be in-

house (Intel and hyperscalers) before broader commercialization. Key milestones include
potential adoption in Google TPU v9, Meta MTIA, and next-generation ASIC platforms,
alongside MediaTek’s ramp in EMIB-T and possible designs on Intel’s advanced nodes
(e.g., 18A).
 Timing and implications for Taiwanese PCB/substrate makers: Intel is
targeting mass production for EMIB-T by H227 or 2028. Our studies suggest the
front-end process could leverage the existing substrate production lines, but the

back-end process would require new equipment investments. The yield rate
challenges will likely lead to higher manufacturing costs at the initial stage, but we
noted customer has been showing strong incentive in EMIB-T technology to
resolve CoWoS capacity bottleneck. Unimicron has been committing resources for
this technology with dedicated production line for Intel. We believe Ibiden and
Unimicron will be the primary supplier for Intel's EMIB-T if volumes takes off.

kevinlu@ lenovo.comkevinlu@ lenovo.com

### Page 13

Taiwan PCB/Substrates22 May 2026 ab 13
Valuation: Poised for more multiple expansion
We note that the prior upcycle in 2019-2022 were primarily driven by the sudden surge
in PC demand where a lot of capacity expansion was than set in motion and substrate
makers had come to agreement with their customers to facilitate capacity expansion.
Unfortunately, consumer demand fell short of expectations coming out of the pandemic
that led to sharp decline in substrate market pricing amid oversupply in 2023-2024. The

substrate market troughed in early 2024 and slowly recovered supported by general
computing demand and moderate PC recovery, later followed by AI taking off with
strength continuing till today. We believe AI is driving a different upcycle for substrate
makers that will be stronger for longer as not only unit growth but also requirement for
larger substrate size and higher layer count will serve as strong demand tailwind.
While the stocks have moved higher, we believe we are still in the early innings of an

upcycle where the stocks were pricing in the initial ASP increases from higher material
costs; but has yet fully factored in a structurally different cycle driven by AI that we
anticipate a much more favourable pricing among imbalance supply/demand dynamics
and multiple should drift higher as margins/earnings start to manifest. Among the
Taiwan substrate makers - 1) we continue to like Unimicron as the clear technology
leader with share gains at NVDA GPU; 2) we maintain Buy on Kinsus as its AI projects

start to bear fruit with share gains at NVDA CPU; and 3) maintain Neutral on NYPCB as
we see volume growth capped as no new capacity additions until 2028 despite stronger
pricing power given more orders based on spot prices that allows NYPCB to enjoy the
full benefit of price hikes sooner than peers.
We are changing our target multiple framework for Unimicron, Kinsus, and Nanya PCB
from a P/BV-based approach to a P/E-based methodology, reflecting improving earnings

visibility and stronger demand support from AI-driven compute intensity and advanced
packaging. Historically, the sector traded on P/BV given earnings volatility, while P/E
appeared elevated during prior cycles (c. 2012–2020) due to depressed earnings. For
asset heavy companies P/B can be good for trough and support valuation or where
earnings are too volatile / not sustainable.  However, the increasing contribution from
advanced substrates has supported more stable profitability. With capacity expansion

more closely aligned to customer demand and higher exposure to high-end applications,
we believe P/E better captures earnings expansion. We think investors will switch to P/E if
they believe the industry has either structural change or longer sustained earnings–
investors that are buying PCB/substrates are likely buying with the view this will be a
stronger for longer cycle vs COVID where they were de-rating by H221 and in this period
are paying higher P/E on expected higher growth the next several years.

dynamic with meaningful uplift in margins; and refresh price targets to factor in more

capacity expansion announcements might lead to oversupply. Ibiden has  noted that

started witnessing on CPUs for general computing/agentic AI and  switch/storage that

Unimicron (Maintain Buy, PT up to NT$1,200) is trading at 30x/19x  2027/2028E P/E,
still below Taiwan hardware/components peers trading average at 33x/26x while at
+1STD P/E historical trading range.  We believe it  has priced in the substrate price
increases to date driven by mostly material price hikes and some pricing premium; yet
considering the industry is entering a structurally different upcycle from AI server

fundamentals with more margin upside  on the way, we believe the stock has not fully
factored in  the stronger pricing bargaining power supported by AI-driven demand
upside and stronger-than-expected margin expansion.
We believe our target multiple of 30x P/E is justified, on par with other industry leading
hardware peers that are riding along the AI secular trend; and also sits at +1STD P/E
historical trading range, which we see warranted by a longer and stronger upcycle

driven by AI fundamentals; and  still view Unimicron  as our top pick among the Taiwan
substrate makers given its leadership. We also note that we previously valued Unimicron
based on P/B valuation methodology with a target multiple of ~7x P/BV. Our updated PT
of NT$1,200 would imply a target multiple of ~9x 2028E P/BV, compared to the prior
peak cycle of 5.0x P/BV. We believe this would be fair as we expect the company to
generate strong and accelerating profits margins with 33.5% ROE in 2028E, up

significantly from 20% average ROE through prior peak cycle 2020-2022.
We believe Unimicron is well-positioned to benefit from AI server as one of the major
substrate suppliers for Nvidia GPU, where the company is gaining market share against
Ibiden; and also supplying major ASICs projects including Google TPU, AWS Trainium,
and Meta MTIA; which we think deserves further-rerating.

Kinsus (Maintain Buy, PT up to NT$680) is trading at 26x/17x 2027E/2028E P/E,
compared to Taiwan hardware/components peers trading average at 33x/26x. We
believe its YTD performance has priced in potential share gains in NVDA CPU as well as

as a reflection point and believe the stock deserves a re-rating as its business structure
starts to improve with  AI-related applications picking up streams to reach 10-15% sales
in 2026/2027E.
We believe our target multiple of 24x 2028 P/E is justified, which is a touch lower than

Taiwan  hardware components trading average at 26x or substrates peers target multiple
(Unimicron/NYPCB target P/E at 30x/25x) considering higher consumer exposure with

(15-30x) of its historical trading range, which we see as warranted as the company is
going through a transformation with strong growth from new AI projects and
networking switch upgrades as well as content increases across AI, regular servers,

valuation methodology with a target multiple of ~4.5x 2027E P/BV. Our updated PT of

expect the company to generate strong and accelerating profits margins with 23.2%
ROE in 2028E, up from 11.2% average ROE through prior peak cycle in 2020-2022.

Major downside risks include 1) substrate margins fall short of expectations; 2) slower
than expected demand for AI GPU/CPU and ASIC projects; and 3) muted consumer
demand  dragging the overall business performance.

fluctuations as a tier two supplier relatively to Ibiden and Unimicron.

Taiwan PCB/Substrates UBS Research
Unimicron (3037.TW, Buy, PT revised up to NT$1200)
Still expect more to come, our top pick among Taiwan substrate makers
Unimicron reported results on April 28th, Q126 GM came in at 18%, improved further
from 15.8% in Q425, driven by stronger sales and favourable product mix with more
high-end ABF product. We model Q226E sales up 9% QoQ with full year 2026E sales to
grow 37% YoY, led by IC carrier +47% YoY and HDI +32% YoY. The company is in the

process of adjusting BT production lines in order to support more ABF demand. We
model BT capacity will  lower by 15% YoY in 2026E while margins likely remain at low
single digits during the transition. Meanwhile, we expect ABF substrate capacity will
increase by 40% YoY in 2026E with Guangfu fab 1 ramping phase III and Yangmei fab 1
capacity to be fully loaded from H126, and noted some incremental capacity additions
coming from continued efforts on automation and reorganizing production lines.

The company previously noted Q126 price hikes were much more meaningful than
Q425, we anticipate price hikes will accelerate into H226E as a number of AI projects,
including GPU/ASIC substrates and  strength from AI CPUs and general server, on top of
limited material supply improvement, should continue to provide supportive pricing
environment. We expect gross margins for ABF products will walk up to 30-35% in
H226 and further improve to 35-40% level in 2027E; HDI margins should gradually tick

up on improving yield rates; while BT margins likely remain below corporate average.

We revise up our 2026/2027E/2028E earnings  by 34%/38%/56% to NT$14.34/NT
$27.54/NT$39.80. We've witnessed content value increase of 50-100%+ each year for

new AI server GPU upgrades -  accelerated product transitions compared to the typical
upgrade cycle of 3-5 years for PCs and traditional servers, and the content step up is also
bigger given the rising component complexities and densities for increasing computing,
power, and  networking requirement.    In addition, we believe there is room for further
margin expansion as the ABF substrate capacity should remain loaded to support robust
AI demand where pricing environment will remain favourable as the industry

undersupply continues into 2026/2027, and the HDI fab is also seeing progress in yield
rate, which should drive stronger profitability. We expect its GM to reach 28-32% in
2026E/2027E, exceeding 2021-2023 upcycle average of 20-25%; and earnings power
should reach NT$39.80 in 2028E, above 2021-2023  upcycle average of NT$10-15/
share.
The downside risks  include: 1) substrate makers' ability to pass on increasing cogs,
which could potentially lower if improved t-glass shortages were able to close the

supply/demand gap; 2) slower-than-expected yield rate improvement for the new HDI
fab that could drag both revenue growth and margins; and 3) lower-than-expected
utilization rate for ABF that could weigh on profitability. We do believe there is ample
room for more upside from AI and margins considering AI servers' substantial growth,
spec upgrades as well as content increases across AI, regular servers, networking/
switches etc; and utilization rate and yield rate improvement for newly ramped

capacities will support margin expansion.

Kinsus (3189.TW, maintain Buy, PT revised up to NT$680)
Maintain Buy on share gains in NVDA CPU and ASICs
We expect utilization rate to remain elevated at 86% for ABF substrates in Q226 with

ABF sales up 18% QoQ. We anticipate growth in both ABF and BT substrates driven by
NVDA CPU, GPU, ASICs (for ABF) and memory (for BT), respectively.  We model ABF
capacity to increase by 22% YoY  in 2026E with low to mid single digit pricing uplifts in
H126 and high single digits increase in H226. Full year we expect Kinsus to deliver 31%
YoY sales growth to factor in new order win and better-than-expected pricing. NVDA VR
will start to ramp in Q326, and expect this new project  could contribute 5-10% of total

revenue in 2026E with 2-3% contribution from ASIC projects. AIl in, we forecast AI-
related ABF substrate revenue contribution could reach 10%-15% in 2026E.
In our prior note, we highlighted its exposure to NVDA/AMD GPUs as well as other ASIC
projects (likely Google and Meta, in our view) and potential share gains into Broadcom
switch. We believe Kinsus has secured  orders for NVDA's Vera CPUs and will likely
become a major supplier with potentially 50% share, up from 10% share for Grace

CPUs. We also note that the company is potentially looking to solidify its relationship
with its customers through strategic investments (such as private placement) to  assure
better order visibility. Meanwhile, we still model muted growth or handset (35% of
sales) and contact lens (15-20% sales). Kinsus' consumer exposure (handset+consumer
electronics+contact lens) will continue to be lower from 70% in 2020 to below 50% by
2027E.

Q226E due to continued momentum in networking and ASIC projects starting to ramp.
We forecast NYPCB sales to grow 40%/44% in 2026E/2027E, supported by structural
demand tailwinds driven by AI/networking and substrate spec upgrades. We expect
ABF/BT price hikes driven by higher material costs will drive margin improvement; and
anticipate ABF substrate will remain undersupply in 2027 with structural demand
tailwinds and further price uplifts. We forecast NYPCB GM/OpM up 11.0ppt/11.6ppt

YoY to 20.1%/16.5% in 2026E and up 6.1ppt/6.4ppt YoY to 26.2%/22.9% in 2027E,
considering the supportive pricing environment to pass on costs and secure capacity.
At its shareholder meeting in May 2026, management noted  it looks to kick off large-
scale capacity expansion and capital investments will exceed historical level. NYPCB
invested a total of NT$44.5bn over 2020-2023 (c.NT$11bn/year on average) and based
on its historical capacity expansion, we estimate every NT$10bn capex investment

would imply c.200-300ksq ft (or roughly 25-35mn units) of ABF substrate monthly
capacity additions. 80% of its current capacity sits in Taiwan with the remaining 20% at
Kunshan. Kunshan fab is expected to see double-digit capacity expansion in 2026
through debottleneck. The new capacity expansion will focus on high-end ABF
substrates, but unlikely to come online before 2028, which could capped on growth.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Investing in the technology sector involves a high degree of risk. Rapid technological changes,
increasing competition and exposure to macroeconomic cycles are among the many risks
faced by investors in tech stocks. Moreover, it is extremely difficult to project the financial
results of tech companies since their operating models are highly volatile and unpredictable.
Finally, valuing tech stocks can prove challenging as neither traditional nor non-traditional

valuation measures provide much insight into how these stocks trade.
Our PT for Unimicron is based on P/E multiple methodology.
Downside risks include weaker AI server/ABF substrate demand, utilization and pricing
pressure from capacity expansion, limited margin upside from long-term contracts, input cost
volatility (ABF, T-glass, CCL), execution risks in overseas expansion, rising competition, and
packaging technology transitions.
Our PT for Kinsus is based on P/E multiple methodology.

Downside risks include lower-than-expected share gains in AI server projects, lower-than-
expected global PC/server demand, oversupply from capacity expansion, raw material cost
increases, intensified competition and a lack of spec upgrades and content increases.
Our price target for Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board is based on P/E multiple methodology.
Upside risks include faster-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switches, stronger-than-
expected pricing increase driving margins back to 2021-22 profitability level, better-than-

expected consumer demand, and continued tightness in BT substrates.
Downside risks include lower-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switch projects, lower-
than-expected global PC/server demand, oversupply from capacity expansions, raw material
cost increases, intensified competition, and a lack of spec upgrades and content increase.
As for industry risks, substrate applications are dominated by PCs, but servers, artificial
intelligence and advanced computing are driving demand growth. We believe key risks to the

industry include slower-than-expected growth in servers, networking, and high performance
computing, ASP pressure, margin erosion, and lack of spec upgrades. We believe the flexible
circuit board and high-density interconnect board segments are more vulnerable to
fluctuations in the consumer product cycle and have lower visibility.
kevinlu@ lenovo.comkevinlu@ lenovo.com

Quantitative Research Review
UBS Global Research publishes a quantitative assessment of its analysts' responses to certain questions about the likelihood of an occurrence
of a number of short term factors in a product known as the 'Quantitative Research Review'.  The views for this month can be found below.
Views contained in this assessment on a particular stock reflect only the views on those short term factors which are a different timeframe to

the 12-month timeframe reflected in any equity rating set out in this note.  For previous responses please make reference to (i) previous UBS
Global Research reports; and (ii) where no applicable research report was published that month, the Quantitative Research Review which can

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

performance record, discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record, discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
At ISCAS 2026 in Shanghai on May 25, Huawei introduced the "t (Tau) Law" - a time-
based scaling principle for multi-layer electronic systems that aims to succeed
Moore's Law as geometric scaling faces limitations. This approach optimizes a
unified time constant (t) across all computing levels—from transistors to data

centers—shifting focus from size reduction to overall system efficiency, including
interconnects, signal integrity, power, and thermal management.
Under this new concept, Huawei has innovated "Logic Folding" — vertically stacking
active layers for digital, analog, and memory circuits, which has achieved a 55%
increase in transistor density and a 41% gain in power efficiency at a fixed process
node.
At the AI cluster level,  t Law, combined with technologies such as a unified memory

semantic bus interconnect architecture, near-package optical Hi-ONE (in-package
high-speed optoelectronic interconnects), and edge-to-surface 3D Folding, is
projected to achieve more than a 100× increase in hardware integration by 2035.
Global AI Transceivers: Industry Demand Likely to be Even Stronger (14 May 2026)
Global Technology: AI Transceivers: Growth Dominates Disruption (26 Feb 2026)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We believe the "t (Tau) Law" is a fundamental theory supporting the exponential
growth of the AI transceiver industry. We reiterate our positive view.
t (Tau) Law is a  framework introduced by Huawei to optimize signal delay (t) to
boost transistor density, energy efficiency, and performance.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

維修與製造動能延續，獲利持續成長 
 飛機製造大廠在手訂單皆超過 11 年，預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 2.65 元：
截至 Apr-26，波音及空巴在手訂單分別達 6,807 架(pre ASC 606)及
8,971 架，以近年交付水準估算，訂單能見度約 11 年以上，顯示民用
航空需求仍具長期支撐。長榮航太 Apr-26 營收 17.09 億元
(+0.64%MoM)，符合預期。展望 2Q26，維修及製造業務需求維持穩
健，預估營收 50.1 億元(+5.7%QoQ)。獲利方面，預估稅後淨利 9.94
億元(+2.9%QoQ)，預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 2.65 元。 
 維修需求與製造訂單支撐 ， 預估2026 年稅後 EPS 9.54 元：展望 2026
年 ， 維修業務可望受惠維修工時提升及價格調整 ，有助支撐營運成長；
製造業務則隨民用航空市場穩健復甦，訂單動能延續，預估2026 年營
收 202.7 億元(+11.5%Y oY)。獲利方面，隨產品組合持續優化，預估毛
利率 24.4%。假設匯率持穩，且長異營運逐步改善並小幅挹注獲利，
預估稅後淨利 35.7 億元(+74.7%Y oY)， 上修2026 年稅後 EPS 9.54 元。 
 維持買進評等，目標價 200 元：評價方面，以 2026 年 EPS 估算，目
前 PE 約為 16X，介於長榮航太過去一年區間 15X~30X 下緣，雖無人
機相關專案推進時程仍待觀察，短期市場觀望情緒仍存，惟考量本業
營運動能延續，仍維持買進建議，目標價 200 元(2026F PE 21X)。

長榮航太客戶分布全球逾 40 家
航空公司，擁有豐富的維修經驗 
 長榮航太於 1997 年 11 月成立，前身為長安航太，於 1999 年更名，目前實收
資本額為 37.46 億元 ， 為長榮航空(2618)持股 55.05%之轉投資公司 。 截至1Q26
底，員工人數超過 3,200 人。主要從事航空器機體、零組件及發動機維修、航
空器零組件製造及組裝等。主要生產基地包括四座飛機維修棚廠、一座發動機
工廠、飛機零組件工廠、補給庫房及辦公中心，且觀音二廠(製造用)預計 4Q26
完工，2027 年投產，有助於後續產能利用提升。以 1Q26 產品別營收比重而
言，航空器維修及其他 79%，航空器之零組件製造銷售 21%。

長異隨出貨量回升， 1Q26 持續
獲利 
 長異為長榮航太與 GE (GE.US)合資成立之子公司，長榮航太持股 49%，主要
從事航空器發動機及零件維修。 2024 年因內部品質控管問題暫停營運，並配
合主管機關及客戶進行重新審核，期間認列庫存減損及存貨相關損失。然自
Dec-24 起逐步復工，隨主管機關及客戶端完 成重新認證後，出貨動能穩步回
升，營運逐步回歸正軌。就營運面而言， 2025 年前三季仍呈虧損，惟虧損幅
度逐季收斂，至 4Q25 已轉虧為盈，1Q26 單季獲利 0.24 億元，顯示產能利用
率明顯提升，接單動能持續改善，並受惠於 GE 供應鏈逐步回穩，整體營運好
轉。

另，就停工前採購之航材及延遲交付所衍生之相關損失， 長榮航太 目前正與
GE 協商虧損分攤方式，考量涉及金額較大，元富預期相關共識將延 至 2H26
逐步明朗。整體而言，隨 GE 引擎需求維持成長動能，且相關損失協商結果具
正向發展空間，預期 2026 年長異營運表現有望延續改善趨勢。

Exhibit 6：長異近年獲利趨勢

Sources : Company，MasterLink 
兩大飛機製造大廠在手訂單皆超
過 11 年，長榮航太營運具長期
成長潛力 
 累計至 Apr-26，波音(BA.US)交付 190 架飛機，年增 15 架，主要受惠 737 系
列月產能提升；空中巴士(EADSY .US) 累計交付 181 架，年減 11 架，推估仍
受引擎等供應鏈因素影響，惟空巴預計 2026 年全年交機目標為 870 架，目前
達成率 20.8%，預期後續隨供應鏈狀況改善，交機狀況有望逐步回升。在供應
鏈瓶頸尚未完全緩解下，兩大飛機製造商在手訂單仍維持高檔，截至 Apr-26，
波音及空巴在手訂單分別達 6,807 架(pre ASC 606)及 8,971 架 ， 以近年交付水
準估算，訂單能見度約 11 年以上，顯示民用航空需求仍具長期支撐。隨供應
鏈狀況逐步改善，預期將持續帶動航太維修及製造需求，長榮航太後續營運動
能可望受惠。

長榮航太 Apr-26 營收 17.09 億元(+0.64%MoM，+11.75%Y oY)，2Q26 營收達
成率 34%，符合預期。展望 2Q26，維修及製造業務需求維持穩健，預估營收
50.1 億元(+5.7%QoQ，+7.3%Y oY)。獲利方面，考量成本及費用控制得宜，
預估毛利率 27.17%，營益率 23.67%；假設匯率維持穩定 ， 預估稅後淨利9.94
億元(+2.9%QoQ，+269.7%Y oY)，預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 2.65 元。

高齡機隊推升維修需求，製造新
案陸續推進 
 根據國際航空運輸協會(IATA)與研調機構 Oliver Wyman 統計，2018 年全球機
隊平均機齡約 11.2 年，然受航太供應鏈吃緊及新機交付延宕影響 ， 航空公司延
後汰換舊機，2025 年全球機隊平均機齡攀升至 15.1 年，創歷史新高。其中，
客機平均機齡達 12.8 年，反映高齡機隊持續推升維修與引擎需求。

維修業務方面，由於桃園機場第三航廈預計 2027 年完工，第三跑道預計 2030
年啟用，在相關基礎建設完成前，長榮航太維修產能仍受一定限制，因此持續
增聘人力以提升服務量能。此外，長榮航太於 May-26 公告承租桃園機場棚廠
前停機棚土地，有助提升飛機維修調度效率，並微幅增加維修量能。

製造業務方面，觀音二廠預計於 4Q26 完工，1Q27 投產。新專案部分，長榮
航太於 2025 年與歐系客戶簽訂新合約項目開發，首件產品預計於 1Q27 以組
裝件形式交付 ， 待3Q27 取得特殊製程認證後 ， 將進一步導入零件自製 ； 此外，
1Q26 取得美系客戶新機型機翼組件項目，預計首件將於 3Q27 交付。

國內無人機需求仍具潛力，後續
標案時程待觀察 
 根據 2025/11/24 國防部軍備局最新公告，規劃採購 48,750 架軍用商規無人
機，分別為沉浸式、投彈式、中程自殺式、小型自殺式及濱海監偵型，整體規
格較 Jul-25 進一步明確。考量長榮航太於 2024 年取得國防部艦載型監偵無人
機專案(約 4.2 億元)，具備相關技術與專案執行經驗 ，又本次標案採複數決標，
並以國內廠商為優先評選對象，預期長榮航太具備一定競爭優勢，若後續標案
推動時程順利，相關訂單最快有望自 2027 年起逐步貢獻營收。

然，短期而言，由於國防特別條例尚未納入國內無人機相關預算，後續專案推
進時程仍具不確定性，相關訂單認列時點仍須持續觀察。除國內軍用項目外，
長榮航太亦持續拓展國際合作與海外商用市場，以分散單一專案進度影響。

維修需求與製造訂單支撐，預估
2026 年稅後 EPS 9.54 元 
 展望 2026 年，維修業務可望受惠既有客戶飛機維修工時提升及價格調整，且
人力持續擴編，有助支撐營運成長；製造業務則隨民用航空市場穩健復甦，訂
單動能延續，預估 2026 年營收 202.7 億元(+11.5%Y oY)。獲利方面，隨產品
組合持續優化，雖 4Q26 將提列年末獎金，仍預估毛利率年增至 24.4%，營業
利益 41.7 億元(+38.9%Y oY)。 假設匯率持穩，且長異營運逐步改善並小幅挹注
獲利， 預估稅後淨利35.7 億元(+74.7%Y oY)， 上修2026 年稅後 EPS 9.54 元。

維持買進評等，目標價 200 元  評價方面，以 2026 年 EPS 估算，目前 PE 約為 16X，介於長榮航太過去一
年區間 15X~30X 下緣，雖無人機相關專案推進時程仍待觀察，短期市場觀望
情緒仍存 ， 惟考量本業營運動能延續 ， 仍維持買進建議， 目標價200 元(2026F 
PE 21X)。

風險提示  1) 兩大飛機製造商交機不如預期；2) 航太供應鏈瓶頸加劇；3) 匯率影響； 
4) 無人機營收認列不如預期；5) 桃園機場第三航廈、第三跑道工程遞延。

設計降規，但急單使單價提升，預估 2Q26 營收季增 14% 
健策 1Q26 EPS 9.53 元，優於預期 2%，主因產品組合優化、漲價效應帶動
毛利率回溫，且伺服器均熱片出貨維持高檔。我們在 5 月初提及取消鍍金水
冷，主要在於鍍金業者的良率及產能不足，水冷板無法提供足夠的量，造成
Tim 2 的 Metal Ti m 取消，避免 Rubin 大幅遞延。改回一片式後因短期急單
效應，單價會較原本的價格大幅度成長，且原本的價格也較 Blackwell 系列
增加 60%以上，仍可帶動營收成長，本中心預估 2Q26 營收 60.5 億元、季
增 14%，下修 17%，毛利率 42.1%、季增 0.3 個百分點，EPS 1 0.64 元、
季增 12%。 
AMD 仍採用兩片式，未來趨勢仍成立 
儘管目前 Nvidia Rubin 系列改採一片式設計，但我們看到 AMD MI45 5 系列
的均熱片方案仍是採用兩片式設計，就如之前報告所敘，該設計的價格比原
本 Rubin 兩片式更高，該產品將於 4Q26 開始放量生產，可望成為動能之一。
我們認為，晶片尺寸變大帶動均熱片尺寸成長的趨勢仍未改變，且未來晶片
量在 AWS、Google ASIC 的帶動下持續增加，對健策的 Ring 或 Lid 出貨量
都有正面效益。預估健策 2027 年營收 492 億元、年增 80%，毛利率 45.9%、
年增 2.3 個百分點，EPS 106.78 元、年增 105%，下修 30%。 
兩件式及 Removable Lid 仍在客戶藍圖中 
我們下修健策 2026/27 年獲利 24%/30%，主要反映規格降低之影響，認為
健策的股價已經充分反映其規格調整的訊息，且因為 AMD 仍採用兩件式，
認為未來兩片式或 Removable Lid 的採用範圍仍會持續提升，且翹曲仍是一
大問題，後續改用兩片式仍有可能，認為 健策的長期趨勢仍未改變 ，維持健
策買進評等，目標價下修 4,270 元係以 2027 年預估每股盈餘、40 倍本益比
評價。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
Rubin 均熱片設計降規，然而但急單使單價提升，預估 2Q26 營收季
增 14%。 
⧫  
AMD MI455 系列的均熱片方案仍是採用兩片式設計，價值含量大幅提
升，可望成為健策動能之一，預估健策 2027 年營收年增 80%。 
⧫  
均熱片規格升級仍為長期趨勢，目標價 4,270 元係以 2027 年 EPS、
40 倍本益比評價。

均熱片出貨維持高檔。我們在 5 月初提及取消鍍金水冷，主要在於鍍金業者的良率及產能不足，水
冷板無法提供足夠的量，造成 Tim 2 的 Metal Tim 取消，避免 Rubin 大幅遞延。改回一片式後因短
期急單效應，單價會較原本的價格大幅度成長，且原本的價格也較 Blackwell 系列增加 60%以上，
仍可帶動營收成長，本中心預估 2Q26 營收 60.5 億元、季增 14%，下修 17%，毛利率 42.1%、季

### Page 3

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：散熱 2026 年 5 月 22 日 Jentech 第3 頁，共 15頁  
Rubin 改回均熱片，翹曲限制放寬 
儘管目前 Nvidia Rubin 系列改採一片式設計，但我們看到 AMD MI455 系列的均熱片方案仍是採用
兩片式設計，就如之前報告所敘，該設計的價格比原本 Rubin 兩片式更高，該產品將於 4Q26 開始
放量生產，可望成為動能之一。 
另外，我們有發現儘管這次緊急改成一片式，但其實在測試上仍有顛簸，主要因為去年研發初期在
使用一片式時，就有發現會有略為明顯的翹曲發生，因此改用兩片式。而這次改成一片式仍有此問
題產生，因此在台積電的翹曲限制有略放寬，讓一片式可達到使用標準。我們認為，這代表大尺寸
晶片的翹曲問題仍存在，一片式在未來不一定是最佳解，後續改用 兩件式及 Removable Lid 才是最
佳選擇。 
我們認為，晶片尺寸變大帶動均熱片尺寸成長的趨勢仍未改變，且未來晶片量在 AWS、Google 
ASIC 的帶動下持續增加，對健策的 Ring 或 Lid 出貨量都有正面效益。預估健策 2027 年營收 492
億元、年增 80%，毛利率 45.9%、年增 2.3 個百分點，EPS 106.78 元、年增 105%。 
圖 3：鍍金水冷板示意圖

資料來源：元大投顧

CPO 交換晶片將採 Ring + Stiffener 設計，2027 年開始放量 
根據調查，預期今年 10 月開始 Spectrum 及 Quantum 5 交換器晶片開始量產，其設計主要是用於
交換器上，預期 2027 年 Quantum 5 晶片量達 280 萬個，若台積電產能允許則 2028 年將再繼續擴
大產量，Spectrum 及 Quantum 5 晶片皆採用 Stiffener 設計，但 Quantum 5 上方還有 3 個 OE 為
一組的環狀片(Ring)。本中心預期健策將成為 Stiffener 主要供應商，2027 年將貢獻營收約 61 億。 
圖 4：Spectrum (LHS)及 Quantum 5 (RHS)

資料來源： Nvidia

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：散熱 2026 年 5 月 22 日 Jentech 第4 頁，共 15頁  
產業概況  
AI、ASIC、通用型強勢出擊，2026 年美系 CSP 資本支出估年增 86%  
在 ChatGPT 等 AI 應用陸續推向市場，各大業者紛紛開始研發自有超級大模型，包括 Gemini 
(Google)、Claude (Anthropic) 、Llama (Meta) 等，各大品牌開始大舉投入大型語言模型(LLM)市場，
且每一個產品的智慧皆持續提高，部分消費者也陸續從 ChatGPT 陣營轉移至 Gemini 陣營，反映
出消費者開始逐漸提升對 AI 的黏著度。根據 Grand View Research 調查，2024 年全球 AI 市場營
收預估約 2,792 億美元，預估 2030 年市場規模超過 3 兆美元，擁有超過 30% 的年複合增長率。 
面對這爆發性的長期成長趨勢，四大 CSP 業者(Amazon、Microsoft、Google、Meta)在法說會上
一致強調，AI 投資已轉變為一項橫跨 2026 年的結構性戰略，CSP 大廠不僅持續擴大對 GB300 等
次世代 AI 伺服器的採購，更將重點放在資料中心基礎設施的長期戰略規劃，以應對 AI 運算對電力
及散熱系統強勁成長的需求，確保未來數年內擁有足夠的能源與容量來支持持續迭代的大模型部署。
本中心認為，2026 年美國四大 CSP 資本支出將年增 86%，且將有一半的成長來自於資料中心建置，
代表了資料中心的不足及未來伺服器需求。明年不僅僅是 NVIDIA GB300 的天下，AWS、Google、
Meta ASIC 的崛起也將是一大關鍵，其中又以 Google TPU 需求最為強勁，已經有兩個客戶預計將
採購 TPU。 
另外，受惠於 AI 訓練與推論需求爆發的連帶效應，本中心預估 2026 年通用型伺服器出貨量將年增
23%，其中以存儲型伺服器需求最為強勁。隨著生成式 AI 應用普及和使用者數量暴增，CSP 等業
者必須不斷擴充容量，且不僅限於高階 GPU 伺服器，龐大的 AI 模型訓練和推論在使用狀態中將消
耗大量的數據(DATA)，使得存儲型伺服器的需求大增。

圖 5：CSP 資本支出預估

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：散熱 2026 年 5 月 22 日 Jentech 第5 頁，共 15頁  
Google TPU 的崛起，ASIC 大幅度成長 
根據供應鏈調查，2026 年美國三大 CSP 業者(Google、AWS、Meta)的自研 AI 晶片出貨量預計較
2025 年持續擴大。其中，Google TPU 的出貨量最為驚人，預計將貢獻 430 萬個，較去年底提升
20%，佔據 ASIC 市場的主導地位。 
本中心預期，ASIC 的大規模部署將大幅拉升液冷散熱的採用比重，除了 AWS 的水冷採用比重預
估在 20-30%外，Google 和 Meta 兩大 CSP 業者皆傾向於全面採用水冷散熱模式，這將直接帶動
水冷供應鏈(如奇鋐、雙鴻、台達電等)的出貨量在 2026 年再次強勁成長。 
在散熱材料端，Google 也在積極探討將傳統導熱介面材料(Tim2)改為使用銦片(Indium)的可能性，
主要在於優化晶片與散熱模組間的熱傳導效率，目前相關設計正在認證中，顯示 CSP 業者對極致
散熱解決方案的投入不斷深化。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：散熱 2026 年 5 月 22 日 Jentech 第6 頁，共 15頁  
獲利調整與股票評價  
重申健策買進評等，目標價 4,270 元 
我們認為一片式仍有翹曲的問題存在，但因為在翹曲限制放寬的情況下，讓一片式可達到使用標準 ，
代表大尺寸晶片的翹曲問題仍存在，一片式在未來不一定是最佳解，後續改用兩件式及
Removable Lid 才是最佳選擇。 
我們下修健策 2026/27 年獲利 24%/30%，主要反映規格降低之影響，認為健策的股價已經充分反
映其規格調整的訊息，且因為 AMD 仍採用兩件式，認為未來兩片式或 Removable Lid 的採用範圍
仍會持續提升，且翹曲仍 是一大問題，後續改用兩片式仍有可能，認為健策的長期趨勢仍未改變，
維持健策買進評等，目標價下修至 4,270 元係以 2027 年預估每股盈餘、40 倍本益比評價。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：散熱 2026 年 5 月 22 日 Jentech 第10 頁，共 15頁

公司簡介  
健策(3653)成立於 1987 年，為台灣領先的精密金屬零組件與散熱解決方案供應商，專注於高階製程能力與客製化設計服務，
公司早期由精密模具與電子零件起家，憑藉著強大的垂直整合能力，涵蓋設計、模具開發、沖壓、塑膠射出及電鍍表面處
理等一條龍製程，成功轉型為散熱領域的領導者。健策主要產品包含各式散熱均熱片、 LED 導線架、連接器等，總部位於
臺灣桃園，並在桃園、中國無錫設有廠區。目前健策的核心產品為高階均熱片 (Heat Spreader) ，營收佔比超過六成，並長
期供應全球半導體巨頭與雲端伺服器大廠。受惠於 AI 伺服器、HPC 及電動車的快速成長，健策積極布局液冷散熱技術(如
微流道蓋板 MCL)與車用 IGBT 散熱模組，並與台積電等頂尖供應鏈維持深度合作，鞏固其在散熱模組及導線架市場的領先
地位。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：散熱 2026 年 5 月 22 日 Jentech 第12 頁，共 15頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 健策整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於中等的位置，
在科技硬體行業中的公司中排名持平同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 健策的整體曝險屬於低等水準，略低於科技硬體行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的  ESG 議
題包含碳自有營運、商業道德和資源使用是公司需要更加注重的議題。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：健策在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬中等。 
 圖 21：ESG 分析

以及風險概況之分析。

以及風險概況之分析。

材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。

壹、投 資 建 議

一. 啟碁為通訊設備網通代工廠，持續調整產品佈局  
啟碁(6285)為網通大廠主要提供通訊設備 ODM 服務，具備天線設
計、軟硬體整合、機構設計、系統整合、介面開發與產品測試等
服務，開發各頻段無線通訊設備，從衛星通訊設備、衛星廣播、
衛星電視等，切入行動通訊、車用工控、智慧家庭、物聯網等產
品線。啟碁依照產品別應用分為，S e r v ic e  P rov id e r 佔 41 % 、
Automotive 與 Industrial 佔 27%、Enterprise 佔 31%、Others 佔 1%。
啟碁產能佔比在台灣佔 35%、越南佔 40%、中國佔 15%，越南二
期約佔 5%、墨西哥廠約佔 5%，預期 2026 年越南廠佔比提升至
50% 。 銷售區域別美洲佔 72% 、 歐洲佔 12% 、 亞洲 16% 。 啟碁認為，
北美與歐洲市場 5G FWA、企業網通設備、低軌衛星設備拉貨增
加、及車用 ADAS 也照計畫進行中，整體而言，2026 年啟碁隨著
網路交換器(高階 Core Switch)進入 800G Switch 產業上升循環，啟
碁預期將有 AI Server 用之高階網路交換器(800G Switch 新品)貢獻
營運，2026 年起 800G Switch 放量佔 2026 年營收達 5%(約當 50~60
億元)，預期 800G Switch 2027 年後營收可望達成 10~15%營收佔
比，啟碁切入 800G Switch 時程較晚以品牌端、企業端用戶為主，
啟碁會開始切入 Data Center 以及 Edge Switch ， 與母公司緯創(3231)
經營市場有所區隔但也部分重疊。展望 2026 年， 啟碁於 OCS(光學
網路交換器)佈局有成。啟碁始導入 OCS(光學網路交換器)，預期
27 年 OCS 會貢獻營收，啟碁 OCS 技術層次類似 Cohr 技術，但應
用層面，因為 Switch 客戶在 AI 架構下面臨高耗能的需求，OCS(光
學網路交換器)導入之後足以降低能耗達 90~95% 。 啟碁採用關鍵的
WSS(Liquid Crystal on Silicon MEMS 結構去做)智慧型光通訊交換
技術，動態管理波長、波長選擇開關，與 MEMS 技術去做調變解
調訊號。

啟碁車用佈局(車用細分為車載資通訊佔大宗、衛星廣播、ADAS、
模組與天線)，汽車電子市場的屬性深耕產品與服務，提供模組感
應器 ADAS 等產品，直接提供服務給電動車車廠客戶，車用部分
也開始出貨北美的車用充電樁(模組、整機)都有出貨，預期車用充
電樁含整機、模組，相當於絕對營收金額 50~100 億元。此外，北
美衛星廣播營運商 Sirius-XM 之車用衛星廣播模組，為啟碁車用耕

耘多年之客戶之一，可望受惠於 SiriusXM 在 2025 年 CES 展場中
指出未來車用衛星廣播、串流和播客領域的最新技術發展，分享

二. 長期企業需求、5G FWA 放量，啟碁仍為受惠廠商 
啟碁過去耕耘天線設計多年、材料選擇、封裝、測試至模組化能
力 ， 微型裝置天線模組化量產能力佳 ， 可望 AiP(Antenna-in-Package)
集成封裝模組競爭中脫穎而出。因客戶生產多元與風散生產基地
考量，啟碁越南產能加入營運，8.3 萬平方米的廠房空間，建置越
南二期廠房，2025~2026 年提升越 南廠的產能。展望 2026 年，啟
碁認為歐洲 5G FWA 量產、美國地區企業網通市佔率有所提升，
受惠北美寬頻基建法案挹注，啟碁於美國也有生產據點，5G FWA
會於 2025 年開始放量且區域來說會是在北美、歐洲為主。2026 年
啟碁北美佔啟碁營收達 67%(未來會將北美地區營收佔比調整至合
適的比重) ， 成長多數是在北美地區 ， 產品線包含 Connected Home、
車用、網通、企業端、低軌、WiFi-7 等產品。啟碁歐洲市場營收
約 12~14%，因歐洲經濟未見起色拉貨偏弱，但有看到其他中國網
通廠商，在歐洲市場供貨遭遇難題，而有看到轉單給台廠的效應。
啟碁提及 5G CPE(包含 Gateway、 Module 、FWA 、Router、光纖
等 )非單指 5G FWA 佔啟碁營收比重 10% ， 受惠於美國基建對於光
纖 CABLE、FWA 都有感受到拉貨，因應 5G FWA 在歐洲市場擴展
有成，2026 年 5G FWA 也會隨之增長。

三.  低軌道衛星對啟碁具正向貢獻，預期毛利率優化 
根據低軌衛星的領先廠商：SpaceX、Telesat、Oneweb、Amazon 均
為台灣網通廠商的客戶或潛在客戶，據供應鏈訪查得知，在低軌
衛星客戶(S 客戶進入產品汰換期 FCC 核准發射總量為第 2 代 
3 萬顆(評估初期會先有 4,000~5,000 顆) 、O 客戶也追加數量至上
千顆、美系 A 客戶期望在 2 年先發射規劃的一半數量)。因此，
宏遠投顧認為 2024~2026 年之間，低軌衛星的總量會從市場原先
預期 1.5~2 萬顆，上修至 3 萬顆+，天上衛星量增對應之地面接
收站量體也會同步增加(評估至少有數百萬座屋頂型地面接收
站) 。 宏遠投顧估計此低軌道衛星之地面接收站商機 ， 對於啟碁 2024
年營收貢獻介於 5~10%之間，在近期低軌衛星量增之下營收比重
接近 10% ，低軌衛星營運比重於 2025~2027 年持續增加中，低軌道
衛星延續 2~3 年進入成長爆發期，對於啟碁營運帶來正向貢獻。 
宏遠投顧認為，低軌衛星發射量可望隨成本下降而提升，衛星地
面接收站體之相位是陣列天線設計有其難度，通訊用天線設計為

波長的 1/4~1/2，隨著傳播頻率愈高，天線長度愈短，在 5G 時代
mmWave 天線集成高頻率通訊、MIMO 載波聚合多天線技術等，對

啟碁此美系客戶 LEO 量增加至少+30~50%YoY。啟碁有整合 LEO
之地面接收站體用之多波束整合型天線(Mu lti Beam Antenna)供應
給其 LEO 客戶所用，預期 LEO 產品線量能增長，對於公司毛利率
有正向挹注。 再者， 啟碁為 S 客戶地面接收站模組之唯一供應商，
訂閱用戶數增長會帶動地面接收站量體增長，預計 2026 年低軌衛
星營運成長+40~50%YoY，依此評估，低軌衛星營收佔 2026 年啟
碁營收可望增長至 15~20%之間。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 325 元 
啟碁科技競爭優勢為天線與 RF 設計能力，專精於寬頻、多媒體、
物聯網通訊領域等應用，也具有無線領域之通訊技術，微波通訊、
數位無線通訊、天線設計、軟體工程、多媒體整合等經驗，低軌
道衛星商機，亦為啟碁一大營運成長機會。展望 2026 年，預估啟
碁年營收為 1351.42 億元，+22.58%YoY，毛利率為 13.66%，稅後
淨利為 59.41 億元，稅後 EPPS+12.28 元；展望 2027 年，預估啟碁
年營收 1537.32 億元，+13.76%YoY，毛利率為 13.60%，稅後淨利
為 69.27 億元 ， 稅後 EPS+14.32 元 。 啟碁企業用 WiFi-6/WiFi-6e(WiFi-7
滲透率於 2024 年將超越 10%，預期 2025 年網通 WiFi-7 佔啟碁營
收比重來到 10~15%，2026 年在企業端 WiFi-7 滲透率將超越 50%) 
AP、企業換機之高階網路交換器(800G Switch)，5G FWA 成為新舊
產品轉換之成長動能。基於網通產業適逢調整期，給予啟碁投資
建議維持買進，目標價 325 元(23.00 x2027 EPS)。

主要提供通訊設備 ODM 服務，具備天線設計、軟硬體整合、機構
設計、系統整合、介面開發與產品測試等服務，開發各頻段無線
通訊設備，從衛星通訊設備、衛星廣播、衛星電視等，切入行動
通訊、車用工控、智慧家庭、物聯網等產品線。

二.25 年營運概況及 26 年展望 
1.啟碁科技產品範疇廣泛，近年來積極提升營運商與 SI 比重 
啟碁科技近年來逐步提升客戶電信營運商與系統整合廠商(SI)比
重，目前為止佔營收比重達到 3 5 ~ 4 0 %，均為直接交貨( D i r e c t  
Account)為主 。 回顧 1H20 啟碁科技車用 、 部分網通產品呈現衰退，
受惠於居家辦公與遠距教學帶動 WiFi Router 需求以及 NB 無線模
組成長。展望 2H20，啟碁認為智慧家庭應用當中，成長最大的專
案是 OTT STB 成長達到 40~50%YoY，從 2Q20 開始 OTT STB 受惠
於疫情增溫 OTT STB 拉貨動能增強 ， 預期拉貨動能延續至 2020 年
底，帶動智慧家庭應用端維持 10~15%YoY 年成長動能。

2.未來 2~3 年為 WiFi-6 換機潮，企業端產品汰換先行放量 
隨著串流影音與 XR(虛擬實境技術)逐漸成熟，使用者對於無線高
頻寬傳輸要求殷切，帶動了 802.11ad、802.11ax、Mesh、5G 等各種
高頻寬無線通訊技術快速成長。未來十年，5G 是開啟移動終端與
萬物互聯之技術開端 ， 市場預期 2010 年全球有 53 億移動終端 ， 到
2020 年達到 107 億，至 2030 年成長到 170 億，尤其是中國市場移
動用戶月流量將持續大幅增長至 2030 年達 130 倍之高，所帶來的
經濟規模十分巨大。此外，根據 Gartner 預估，Wi-Fi 6 企業與中
小型商務用戶產值將從 2019 年 2.5 億美元，到 2023 年 52.2 億美
元，年複合成長率達+114%，國內生產 WiFi-6 產品網通業者包含
啟碁、正文、明泰、神準、合勤控等廠商，均有機會受惠於 WiFi-6
企業端產品先行汰換的換機潮，預期將可持續 2~3 年待 WiFi-6 產
品價格下降落至消費者願意換機之甜蜜價格區間。

圖四、WiFi-6 換機潮為未來 2~3 年內趨勢

### Page 2

2

公司簡介：晶碩為台灣專業隱形眼鏡製造廠商， 營運模式上採自有品牌和代工雙軌
併行 ，1Q26 年品牌和代工營收比重為 17.8%及 82.2%，營收依區域佔比分別為其
他亞洲 78.24%、台灣 14.68%及其他 7.09%，其他亞洲中，日本約佔 65%，中國
15%，台灣競爭同業為海昌、精華光學、望隼、視陽等，國際競爭同業為嬌生、博
士倫等。 
費用控管得宜，1Q 獲利表現優於預期：晶碩 1Q 營收 18.9 億元(-2.3%QoQ、
+18.3%YoY)，毛利率 53.3%，稅後淨利 4.72 億元(+10.2%QoQ、-3.1%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 6.05 元，表現優於預期，主因費用控管得宜，拉升1Q 營利率至 28.8%，
較過去幾季約落於 25-27%高，另單季稅率 15.6%，也低於原預期之 20%。 
日本及歐洲市場矽水膠放量，營運展望樂觀，2026 年預估營收將成長 10-15%：
就各市場目前市況，日本部分，除矽水膠日拋訂單持續成長外，高階品產品包括散
光及多焦系列需求也再增加，彩片部分， 除既有水膠品項因應客戶需求持續推出新
圖紋外，高階產品也有彩片系列商品上市下，預估日本市場今年營收將成長 15-
20%，動能明確，中國部分，因陸廠積極擴產，故在價格競爭力下滑下 (和當地廠
相比，晶碩加計稅與運費等成本，產品報價價差近20%)，2025 年營運較為辛苦，
因應此，產能端晶碩除在中國南通廠進行適度擴產外，越南廠 2H27 也會接棒投
產，因越南輸中國地區免關稅加上運輸上陸運即可下，可有效縮小報價差異，另產
品端，2-3Q26 矽水膠等高階產品將取證，產品定位有機會突破價格廝殺區塊下，
中國市場今年將力拼持平至小幅成長，歐洲市場則在矽水膠系列產品項更趨完整
下，客戶訂單量持續增加中，台灣部分，除自有品牌外，也新增韓系品牌代工下，
預估今年營收將可維持穩健。整體而言，各市場營運展望多穩健至正向，預估晶碩
2026 年營收可達 80.1 億元(+13.7%YoY)。 
產能配置規畫 ：目前月產能約 1.05-1.1 億片 ， 預計至年底將擴充至1.15-1.2 億片 ，
配置上，隨矽水膠出貨量增，大溪廠將持續擴充矽水膠產能，龜山廠則維持現狀，
越南廠則預計 2H27 投產，第一期月產能設定在每月 0.1-0.15 億片。 
財務預估：晶碩 2025 年營收 70.4 億元(+3.3%YoY)，毛利率 52.3%，稅後淨利
16.3 億元(-11.1%YoY)，稅後 EPS 20.87 元，預計配發現金股利 10 元，目前殖利
率約 3.3%。預估 2026 年營收 80.1 億元(+13.7%YoY)，毛利率因經濟規模與匯率
友善 ， 由53.7%上修至 54.5%， 稅後淨利19.4 億元(+18.7%YoY)， 稅後EPS 24.86
元(原估 22.37 元)。
